--- entity_google_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
review_id: 2000
entity_slug: the-office-lounge-foley
google_review_name: places/ChIJJ-XeBMwPmogRRbUCm00NSGU/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2pKVWNFVk9ObWxYYVZjM1dXWkNYMHM0VWsxUmIxRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: The only place in Foley or anywhere nearby that has enough pool tables to be able to actually play pool, and not have to lay your coins on the table to claim it for your next game. Anywhere else you go, there's usually 1 or 2 tables and that is it. And you know you are never gonna get a turn. It is a little crowded on weekends, but still plenty of tables to where you won't have to wait long to get one. My husband and I go maybe once a month to have a couple drinks and to play pool. There are also pool tournaments held there, which I don't know very much about. There are a few of those casino slot machines that you play on and earn points. But everyone is really friendly. *Just a little note: If you don't like cigarette smoke or can't be around it, I would not suggest this place on a busy night. I have a pretty sensitive nose, and I can usually get used to the smoke smell after a few minutes. BUT, when we leave and get back into the car, I can smell the smoke smell on my clothes. I don't know much about all the bands and music and when they play, I know they do karaoke, just not sure if it's a certain day every week or random.

rating: 5
review_text: Came here out of boredom, in search of cheap beer. Was not disappointed. Can't beat $2 bottles of Miller Lite during happy hour.

Definitely a locals bar. I'm introverted and from out of town. It felt like everyone was staring at me when I walked in the door. Barmaid seemed nice enough. They allow smoking, and I'm OK with that. The place is huge. Lots of pool tables, a few coin-op dartboards and gambling machines, a jukebox, a stage, and a snack vending machine (the only food I saw offered here).

I'm honestly not much of a bar person or a pool player, I just wanted to get out for a bit. This was fine.

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: 5b05479d-0484-4879-9058-c5dc9dcff17c
entity_slug: the-office-lounge-foley
reviewer_name: Alison Lanier
rating: 5
body: The only place in Foley or anywhere nearby that has enough pool tables to be able to actually play pool, and not have to lay your coins on the table to claim it for your next game. Anywhere else you go, there's usually 1 or 2 tables and that is it. And you know you are never gonna get a turn. It is a little crowded on weekends, but still plenty of tables to where you won't have to wait long to get one. My husband and I go maybe once a month to have a couple drinks and to play pool. There are also pool tournaments held there, which I don't know very much about. There are a few of those casino slot machines that you play on and earn points. But everyone is really friendly. *Just a little note: If you don't like cigarette smoke or can't be around it, I would not suggest this place on a busy night. I have a pretty sensitive nose, and I can usually get used to the smoke smell after a few minutes. BUT, when we leave and get back into the car, I can smell the smoke smell on my clothes. I don't know much about all the bands and music and when they play, I know they do karaoke, just not sure if it's a certain day every week or random.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true

reviewer_name: S D
rating: 5
body: Came here out of boredom, in search of cheap beer. Was not disappointed. Can't beat $2 bottles of Miller Lite during happy hour.

Definitely a locals bar. I'm introverted and from out of town. It felt like everyone was staring at me when I walked in the door. Barmaid seemed nice enough. They allow smoking, and I'm OK with that. The place is huge. Lots of pool tables, a few coin-op dartboards and gambling machines, a jukebox, a stage, and a snack vending machine (the only food I saw offered here).

I'm honestly not much of a bar person or a pool player, I just wanted to get out for a bit. This was fine.
